2025-06-15T00:50:57.0776720Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0776871Z <pr_title>
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0777391Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0778040Z </pr_title>
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0778231Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0778388Z <pr_description>
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0779580Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0780766Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0780861Z ## Problem
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0781227Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0781711Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0782023Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0782351Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0782683Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0783078Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0783415Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0783560Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0784093Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0784757Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0784848Z ## Solution
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0784980Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0785090Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0785473Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0785781Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0786814Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0788353Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0789508Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0790249Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0790422Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0790924Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0791255Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0791409Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0791779Z ```bash
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0792681Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0793694Z ```
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0793870Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0794034Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0794448Z ```bash
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0795666Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0797106Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0797388Z ```
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0797490Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0797646Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0797902Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0798003Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0798601Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0799345Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0799429Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0799681Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0800432Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0800960Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0801445Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0802010Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0802326Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0802707Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0803354Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0803918Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0804109Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0804124Z 
2025-06-15T00:50:57.0804284Z </pr_description>
